## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

देवा᳓ ह वै᳓ सत्रं᳓ नि᳓षेदुः श्रि᳓यं गच्छेम य᳓शः स्यामान्नादाः᳓ स्यामे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now, once on a time, the gods sat down (to perform) a satra, (sacrificial session), thinking "May we attain excellence, may we attain fame and may we be eaters of food".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा᳘ ह वै᳘ सत्रं नि᳘षेदुः श्रि᳘यं गच्छेम य᳘शः स्यामान्नादाः᳘ स्यामे᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓षांहान्ना᳓द्यमभि᳓जितं त᳓द्धैभ्योऽपचिक्रमिषां᳓चकार ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That same food gained by them wished to get away from them.
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/22 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

पश᳓वो वा᳓ अन्ना᳓द्यं पश᳓वो᳓ऽन्नं ते᳓ हैभ्यो ऽपचिक्रमिषां᳓ [[चक्रु᳓र्य᳓द्वै|चक्रु᳓र्य᳓द्वै᳓]] न इमे᳓ श्रान्ता᳓ न᳓ हिंस्युः᳓ कथं᳓स्विन्नः सक्ष्यन्त इ᳓ति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Food being cattle, it was the cattle which wanted to get away from them (thinking) "These gods are tired and they may eat us up; (can't say) how they will treat us".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

पश᳘वो वा᳘ अन्ना᳘द्यं पशवो᳘ऽन्नं ते᳘ हैभ्यो ऽपचिक्रमिषां᳘ चक्रुर्य᳘द्वै न इमे᳘ श्रान्ता न᳘ हिꣳस्युः᳘ कथ᳘ꣳ᳘स्विन्नः सक्ष्यन्त इ᳘ति ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दु वै᳓ देवा᳓ अस्पृण्वत त᳓द्गृहे᳓षु न्य᳙यच्छन्त त᳓थैभ्य एत᳓दन्ना᳓द्यं ना᳓पाक्रामत्त᳓थो वा᳓ एत᳓ आसते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This the gods saw through and they secured them in the houses. Hence this food (cattle) did not get away from them.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु वै᳘ देवा᳘ अस्पृण्वत त᳘द्गृहे᳘षु᳘ न्ययच्छन्त त᳘थैभ्य एत᳘दन्ना᳘द्यं ना᳘पाक्रामत्त᳘थो वा᳘ एत᳘ आसते
</details>

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ जुहोतीह᳓ र᳓तिरिह᳓ रमध्वमि᳓ति पशू᳓नेवै᳙त᳓दाहेह᳓ धृ᳓तिरिह᳓ स्व᳓धृतिः स्वा᳓हे᳓ति पशू᳓नेवै᳙त᳓दाह ॥
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ द्विती᳓यां जुहोत्युपसृज᳓न्धरु᳓णं मात्र᳓ इ᳓त्यग्नि᳓मेवै᳙त᳓दस्यै᳓ पृथिव्या᳓ उपसृज᳓न्नाह धरु᳓णो मात᳓रं ध᳓यन्नि᳓ति ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/04/06/9/23_atha_dvitIyAM_juhIti.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

9. And the second he offers with, 'Letting the sucking calf to the mother,'--he means to say by this, 'letting the fire go to the earth;'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he makes the second offering (saying) "Letting the suckling calf to the mother"— he says this only by way of letting fire go to this earth.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(न्ते᳘ ऽथ) अ᳘थ द्विती᳘यां जुहीति॥  
(त्यु) उपसृज᳘न्धरु᳘णं मात्र ऽइ᳘त्यग्नि᳘मे᳘वैत᳘त्पृथिव्या᳘ ऽउपसृज᳘न्नाह धरु᳘णो मात᳘रं ध᳘यन्नि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ द्विती᳘यां जुहीति॥  
उपसृज᳘न्धरु᳘णम् मात्र इ᳘त्यग्नि᳘मेॗवैत᳘त्पृथिव्या᳘ उपसृज᳘न्नाह धरु᳘णो मात᳘रं ध᳘यन्नि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

[[टीका न लब्धा।]]
</details>

<details><summary>हरिस्वामी</summary>

**अथ द्वितीयामि**ति [^१_२६५] । अग्निमेवैतत्सवनीयं मातृभूतायाः पृथिव्याः पानायेति शेषः । पृथिव्याः पानायोपसृजन्नाह । किमाह 'रायस्पोषमस्मासुदीधरत्' धारयतु त एवं वा ऽध्वर्य्यौ कुर्वन्ति । सत्रिणः पशून्पश्चिमेन द्वारेण हविर्द्धानं प्रवेशयन्तीत्यर्थः । किं कारणं पुरस्तात्पूर्वस्या दिशः 'प्रत्यञ्चस्तंस्यमानाः' करिष्यमाणाः सोमनिधानार्थं प्रपद्यन्ते । 'अथ' शब्दो विशेषार्थः । 'अथैवं' विपरीतं 'सत्रोत्थाने' प्रपद्यन्ते प्रक्रमसमाप्त्यर्थे लक्षणयेत्यभिप्रायः ॥ ९ ॥ १० ॥

[^१_२६५]: अपरामुपसृजन्निति । उत्तरेण परिक्रम्यापरेण हविर्द्धानं प्रविश्य । का. श्रौ. सू. १२ । ७६ । ७७ ।
</details>
</details>
</div>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/22 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अग्नि᳓मेवै᳙त᳓दिमां᳓ पृथिवीं᳓ ध᳓यन्तमाह रायस्पो᳓षमस्मा᳓सु दीधरत्स्वा᳓हे᳓ति पश᳓वो वै᳓ रायस्पो᳓षः [[पशूनस्मा᳓सु|पशू᳓नस्मा᳓सु]] दीधरदि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

By saying 'a suckling calf drinking from the mother' he only refers to the fire suckling the (moisture of the) earth. (He says) "May he maintain increase of wealth among us—Svāhā". Increase of wealth is cattle and he only means 'let us be enriched with cattle'.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## ०९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓ प्रा᳓ञ्चो निःसृ᳓प्यो᳓त्तरेण परी᳓त्य पश्चा᳓त्प्रा᳓ञ्चो हविर्धा᳓नं प्र᳓पद्यन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They walk out by the eastern (door) and moving to the north, reach the Havirdhāna cart from behind towards the front.
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ उ᳓त्तरस्य हविर्धा᳓नस्य द᳓क्षिणां कूबरी᳓मभिप᳓द्य सत्र᳓स्य᳓र्धिरि᳓ति त᳓त्सा᳓माभि᳓गायति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He having reached the hind shaft of the northern Havirdhāna cart, sings the Sāman called Satrasya ṛddhih (the completion of satra).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓गन्म ज्यो᳓तिरमृ᳓ता अभूमे᳓ति ज्यो᳓तिर्ह्ये᳙ते᳓ य᳓न्ति ये᳓ सत्र᳓मा᳓सतेऽमृ᳓ता ह्ये᳙ते᳓ भ᳓वन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"We have reached the light; we have become immortal because those who sit down in the satra do reach the light and they indeed, become immortal".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

दि᳓वं पृथिव्या᳓ अध्या᳓रुहामे᳓ति दि᳓वंह्ये᳙ते᳓ पृथिव्या᳓ आरो᳓हन्ति ये᳓ [[सत्र᳓मा᳓सते᳓|सत्र᳓मा᳓सते]] विदाम देवा᳓नि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"To the sky have we ascended from the earth" for, they who sit down in the Satra yāga indeed, ascend from earth to sky.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓न् ह्ये᳙ते᳓ विन्द᳓न्ति ये᳓ सत्र᳓मा᳓सते [[स्वर्ज्यो᳓तिरि᳓ति|स्व᳙र्ज्योतिरि᳓ति]] [[निध᳓नमुपा᳓वयन्ति᳓|निध᳓नमुपा᳓वयन्ति]] स्व᳓र्ह्ये᳙ते᳓ य᳓न्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"We have reached to the gods" for, they do attain to the gods, who sit down in the Satra yāga, "To the heavens, to the light" (thus saying) they reach the finale.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा᳘न् ह्येते᳘ विन्द᳘न्ति ये᳘ सत्रमा᳘सते᳘ स्वर्ज्यो᳘तिरि᳘ति निध᳘नमुपा᳘वयन्ति᳘ स्व᳘र्ह्येते य᳘न्ति
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Those who sit down in the Satra yāga do go to the heavens; they become 'light'. Therefore they make the finale by the words 'to the heavens; to the light'.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ यः᳓ सत्र᳓स्य᳓र्धिमभिगा᳓यति रा᳓द्धिमेवै᳙भ्य एत᳓दा᳓शास्ते [[राध्नुव᳓न्त्येवै᳙त᳓त्त्रिरभिगी᳓य|राध्नुव᳓न्त्येवै᳙त᳓त्त्रि᳓रभिगी᳓य]] [[साम|सा᳓म]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He who sings the glory of the Satra yāga, he only prays for prosperity from then. Thus singing with these three Sāmans, they indeed, prosper.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ द᳓क्षिणस्य हविर्धा᳓नस्याधो᳙ऽधो᳓क्षं प्रा᳓ञ्चो निः᳓सर्पन्ति युवं᳓ त᳓मिन्द्रा पर्वता पुरोयु᳓धा यो᳓ नः पृतन्या᳓द᳓प तं᳓ तमि᳓द्धतं᳓ व᳓ज्रेण तं᳓ तमि᳓द्धत᳓म् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then they creep along right under the axle of the southern Havirdhāna cart towards east (saying) "O Indra and Parvata, leaders (of the army) whoever is (going) away from our army, each such one, you scorch with the thunderbolt, scorch him. Frightened and hiding (steelthily), if they run away prattling (in fear) even into whole forests, such enemies of ours, O brave ones, see them destroyed".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ द᳘क्षिणस्य हविर्धा᳘नस्याधोऽधो᳘क्षं प्रा᳘ञ्चो निः᳘सर्पन्ति युवं त᳘मिन्द्रा पर्वता पुरोयु᳘धा यो᳘ नः पृतन्याद᳘प तं᳘ तमि᳘द्धतं व᳘ज्रेण तं᳘ तमि᳘द्धतम् ।
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/44 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

दू᳓रे चत्ता᳓यच्छन्त्सद्ग᳓हनं [[यदि᳓|य᳓दि]] [[नक्षदस्मा᳓कंश᳓त्रून्प᳓रि|न᳓क्षदस्मा᳓कंश᳓त्रून्प᳓रि]] शूर विश्व᳓तो दर्मा᳓ दर्शीष्ट विश्व᳓त इ᳓त्ये᳓तया᳓तिच्छन्दसा [[सर्वं᳓|स᳓र्वं]] वा᳓ अ᳓त्य᳓तिच्छन्दाः [[स᳓र्वेणैवै᳙तत्पाप्मा᳓नम᳓पघ्नते|स᳓र्वेणैवै᳙त᳓त्पाप्मा᳓नम᳓पघ्नते]] त᳓थैनान्पाप्म᳓नोऽभ्य᳓त्येत्य᳓थ य᳓द᳓धोऽधो᳓क्षं व᳓ज्रो वा᳓ अ᳓क्षो व᳓ज्रेणैवै᳙त᳓त्पाप्मा᳓नम᳓पघ्नते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus with the Aticchandas (verses in redundant metre) (they address). All these are verses in Aticchandas. By all these they destroy the enemy. Having thus overcome the enemy, they move underneath the axle (of the Havirdhāna cart). The axle is a thunderbolt and it is by the thunderbolt they kill the enemy. Then the enemy (sin) does not overtake them.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

दू᳘रे चत्ता᳘यच्छन्त्सद्ग᳘हनं यदि᳘ नक्षदस्मा᳘कꣳश᳘त्रून्प᳘रि शूर विश्व᳘तो दर्मा᳘ दर्शीष्ट विश्व᳘त इत्ये᳘तया᳘तिच्छन्दसा सर्वं᳘ वा अत्य᳘तिच्छन्दाः स᳘र्वेणै᳘वैतत्पाप्मा᳘नम᳘पघ्नते त᳘थैनान्पाप्म᳘नोऽभ्य᳘त्येत्य᳘थ य᳘द᳘धोऽधो᳘क्षं व᳘ज्रो वा अ᳘क्षो व᳘ज्रेणै᳘वैत᳘त्पाप्मा᳘नम᳘पघ्नते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/22 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓थैनान्पाप्म᳓नोऽभ्य᳓त्येति स᳓ य᳓था᳓हिस्त्वचो᳓ निर्मुच्ये᳓तैवं᳓स᳓र्वस्मात्पाप्म᳓नो [[निर्मुच्य|निर्मु᳓च्य]] प्रा᳓ञ्चो निः᳓सर्पन्ति प्रा᳓ञ्चः ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus, just as the serpent would cast away its slough, having got rid of all sins, they move on by the eastern side.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

निःसृ᳓प्यो᳓त्तरेण परी᳓त्य पुर᳓स्तात्प्रत्य᳓ञ्चः स᳓दः प्र᳓सर्पन्ति पश्चा᳓द्वै᳓ प्रा᳓ञ्चस्तंस्य᳓माना अ᳓थैवं᳓सत्रो᳓त्था᳓ने ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having emerged at the east, going by the northern side, from the front, they enter to the back side of the sadas. For, from the back towards the front (they moved) when about to perform the sacrifice but thus (they do from the front to the back) while rising from the session.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

निःसृप्यो᳘त्तरेण परी᳘त्य पुर᳘स्तात्प्रत्य᳘ञ्चः स᳘दः प्र᳘सर्पन्ति पश्चाद्वै प्रा᳘ञ्चस्तꣳस्य᳘माना अ᳘थैवꣳ᳘सत्रोत्था᳘ने ॥१२॥

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓ यथाधिष्ण्यं᳓ व्यु᳓पविशन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They sit by their respective dhisnyas (hearths).
</details>

ते᳘ यथाधिष्ण्यं व्यु᳘पविशन्ति

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓दुद्गाता᳓ सा᳓माभि᳓गायति सर्पराज्ञ्या᳓ ऋक्षु᳓ स्वयं᳓ [[प्रस्तुतम᳓नुपगीतं|प्र᳓स्तुतम᳓नुपगीतं]] य᳓थान्यो᳓ नोपशृणुया᳓द᳓ति ह रेचयेत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then the Udgātr sings the Sāman of the Ṛks pertaining to the serpent queen, initiating it himself and inaudiable, so that another would not join him in singing and another would not over hear his singing.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दुद्गाता सा᳘माभि᳘गायति सर्पराज्ञ्या᳘ ऋक्षु᳘ स्वयं प्रस्तुतम᳘नुपगीतं य᳘थान्यो᳘ नोपशृणुयाद᳘ति ह रेचयेत्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓दन्यः᳓ प्रस्तुया᳓द᳓ति ह रेचयेद्य᳓दन्य᳓ उरुगाये᳓द᳓ति ह रेचयेद्य᳓दन्य᳓ उपशृणुया᳓त्त᳓स्मात्स्वयं᳓ [[प्रस्तुतम᳓नुपगीतं|प्र᳓स्तुतम᳓नुपगीतं]] य᳓थान्यो᳓ नोपशृणुया᳓त् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If another is to initiate, it would be in excess for the sacrifice; if another sings with him in chorus, it would be in excess and if another were to overhear it would be in excess. Therefore it is self-initiated, un-accompanied by another and inaudible to be overheard by another.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘दन्यः᳘ प्रस्तुयाद᳘ति ह रेचयेद्य᳘दन्य᳘ उरुगा᳘येद᳘ति ह रेचयेद्य᳘दन्य᳘ उपशृणुयात्त᳘स्मात्स्वयं प्रस्तुतम᳘नुपगीतं य᳘थान्यो᳘ नोपशृणुयात् ॥१३॥
</details>

## १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ य᳓दुद्गाता᳓ सा᳓माभिगा᳓यति वाचो᳓ ह वै᳓ र᳓सोऽभि᳓जितो देवा᳓नांस᳓ इमां᳓ [[पृथिवीं|पृथिवीं᳓]] परा᳙डतिसिसृप्सांचकार ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

इयं᳓ वै᳓ पृथिवी᳓ वा᳓क्त᳓स्या अस्या᳓ र᳓स ओ᳓षधयो व᳓नस्प᳓तयो य᳓दिद᳓मस्या᳓मन्ना᳓द्यंस᳓ इमां᳓ पृथिवीं᳓ प᳓राङतिसिसृप्सां᳓चकार त᳓मेते᳓न सा᳓म्नाप्नुवन्त्स᳓ आप्त᳓ आ᳓वर्तत ता᳓ इमा᳓ अस्या᳓ ऊर्ध्वा᳓ ओ᳓षधयो [[व᳓र्धन्त|वर्धन्त]] ऊर्ध्वा᳓ व᳓नस्प᳓तयः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That essence of the vāk wished to creep away ahead of this earth (by-passing this earth). This earth, forsooth, is the vāk. From her (come out) these plants and trees, which constitute food in her (earth). That essence of the vāk now wished to creep away ahead of this earth. That (essence) they obtained by this Sāman (made up of Ṛks belonging to serpent queen). Thus won, that (essence of vāk) returned (to the earth). That is why these plants and trees grow upwards (from this earth).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

इयं वै᳘ पृथिवी वाक्त᳘स्या अस्या र᳘स ओ᳘षधयो व᳘नस्प᳘तयो य᳘दिद᳘मस्या᳘मन्ना᳘द्यꣳस᳘ इमां᳘ पृथिवीं प᳘राङतिसिसृप्सां᳘चकार त᳘मेते᳘न सा᳘म्नाप्नुवन्त्स᳘ आप्त आ᳘वर्तत ता᳘ इमा᳘ अस्या᳘ ऊर्ध्वा ओ᳘षधयो व᳘र्धन्त ऊर्ध्वा व᳘नस्प᳘तयः ॥१४॥
</details>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓थो वा᳓ एते᳓षां वाचो᳓ र᳓सोऽभि᳓जितः स᳓ इमां᳓ पृथिवीं᳓ प᳓राङ᳓तिसिसृप्सतीयं᳓ वै᳓ पृथिवी᳓ वा᳓क्त᳓स्या अस्या᳓ र᳓स ओ᳓षधयो व᳓नस्प᳓तयो य᳓दिद᳓मस्या᳓मन्ना᳓द्यंस᳓ इमां᳓ पृथिवीं᳓ प᳓राङ᳓तिसिसृप्सति त᳓मेते᳓न सा᳓म्नाप्नुवन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

In like manner, does the essence of the vāk wishes to desert these (Yajamānas) who have gained it and wishes to creep away ahead of them (leaving them behind)—for vāk is this earth and her essence are these plants and trees which constitute food in this. That essence wishes to creep away ahead (leaving this earth behind).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘थो वा᳘ एते᳘षां वाचो र᳘सोऽभि᳘जितः स᳘ इमां᳘ पृथिवीं प᳘राङ᳘तिसिसृप्सतीयं वै᳘ पृथिवी वाक्त᳘स्या अस्या र᳘स ओ᳘षधयो व᳘नस्प᳘तयो य᳘दिद᳘मस्या᳘मन्ना᳘द्यꣳस᳘ इमां᳘ पृथिवीं प᳘राङ᳘तिसिसृप्सति त᳘मेते᳘न सा᳘म्नाप्नुवन्ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/10 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ आप्त᳓ आ᳓वर्तते ता᳓ इमा᳓ अस्या᳓ ऊर्ध्वा᳓ ओ᳓षधयो [[व᳓र्धन्ते|वर्धन्ते]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

By means of this Sāman, they overtake it. Thus overtaken, it (that essence) returns to them.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ आप्त आ᳘वर्तते ता᳘ इमा᳘ अस्या᳘ ऊर्ध्वा ओ᳘षधयो व᳘र्धन्ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ऊर्ध्वा᳓ व᳓नस्प᳓तयः स᳓ यः᳓ सर्पराज्ञ्या᳓ ऋक्ष्वि᳙यं᳓ वै᳓ सर्परा᳓ज्यमन᳓यैवै᳓तत्स᳓र्वमाप्नुवन्ति᳓ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Hence upwards on this earth, grow the plants, upwards the trees. They sing the verses of the serpent queen, for, the queen of serpents is this earth. By means of her they obtain everything.
</details>

## १६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ च᳓तुर्होतॄन्हो᳓ता व्या᳓चष्टे त᳓द्धो᳓तुरेव᳓ व्याख्या᳓नंहो᳓ता हि᳓ स्तुत᳓मनुशं᳓सति य᳓दि हो᳓ता न᳓ विद्या᳓द्गृह᳓पतिर्व्या᳓चक्षीत ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The Hotṛ explains the (stotra called) four-Hotṛs. It is an explanation (to be done) by the Hotṛ himself, because the Hotṛ follows up the stuta (stotra) by that śāstra—if he does not know that, let the Gṛhapati do it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/26 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ वाकोवाक्यं᳙ ब्रह्मो᳓द्यं [[वदन्त्यध्वर्यु᳓मामन्त्र्य|वदन्त्यध्वर्यु᳓माम᳓न्त्र्य]] त᳓दध्वर्योः᳓ प्रतिगरो᳓ ऽरात्सुरिमे᳓ य᳓जमाना भद्र᳓मेभ्यो᳓ य᳓जमानेभ्यो ऽभूदि᳓ति रा᳓द्धिमेवै᳙भ्य एत᳓दा᳓शास्ते राध्नुव᳓न्त्येवै᳙त᳓त् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now they utter Brahmodya (metaphysical investigation on Brahman) which is in the form of vākovākya (statements and counter statements i.e. dialogue), after calling the Adhvaryu. Adhvaryu replies—"These Yajamānas have achieved fulfilment. Merit has come to these Yajamānas"—This is only a wish for fulfilment. These do indeed, fulfill.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ य᳓द्वाकोवाक्यं᳙ ब्रह्मो᳓द्यं व᳓दन्ति स᳓र्वं वा᳓ एत᳓ आप्नुव᳓न्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

By uttering the Brahmodya by way of the vākovākya they achieve everything; they come to possess everything;
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/33 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓वारुत्सता᳓शंसिषुर् ऋ᳓चस्त᳓त्ता᳓ आपंस्ता᳓ अ᳓वारुत्सत प्रा᳓चारिषुर्य᳓जुर्भिस्त᳓त्ता᳓न्यापं [[स्तान्य᳓वारुत्सता᳓थैषा|स्तान्य᳓वारुत्सता᳓थैषा᳓]] मे त᳓द᳓नाप्तं भ᳓वत्य᳓नवरुद्धं य᳓द्वाकोवाक्यं᳙ ब्र᳓ह्मा त᳓देवै᳙त᳓दाप्नुव᳓न्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

they have recited with the Ṛks. Thereby they have attained so much, they have come to possess so much, they have performed with Yajus, there by they have attained so much, they have come to possess so much. But this has not been attained by them, not yet possessed; namely the Brahman (through) vākovākya.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘वारुत्सता᳘शꣳ सिषुर्ऋ᳘चस्तत्ता᳘ आपस्ताꣳ अ᳘वारुत्सत प्रा᳘चारिषुर्य᳘जुर्भिस्तत्ता᳘न्यापꣳ स्तान्य᳘वारुत्सता᳘थैषा मे तद᳘नाप्तं भ᳘वत्य᳘नवरुद्धं य᳘द्वाकोवा᳘क्यं ब्र᳘ह्मा त᳘दे᳘वैत᳘दाप्नुव᳘न्ति
</details>

## १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थौ᳓दुम्बरीं समार᳓भ्य स᳓र्वे वाचंयमा᳓ आसत [[आ᳓स्तमेतोरादित्या᳓द्वा᳓ग्वै᳓|आ᳓स्तमे᳓तोरादित्या᳓द्वा᳓ग्वै᳓]] यज्ञ᳓स्त᳓देत᳓द्यज्ञं᳓ नि᳓र्धयन्ति वि᳓दुहन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Starting from the Udumbara post, they all sit down in silence till the sun sets. The sacrifice is vāk and they milk the sacrifice, extract the essence of it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓था᳓स्तमित आदित्ये᳓ प्रा᳓ञ्चो निःसृ᳓प्या᳓ग्रेण हविर्धा᳓नमासते ता᳓न्वाचंयमा᳓नेव᳓ वाचंयमः᳓ प्रतिप्रस्थाता᳓ वसतीव᳓रीभिः प᳓रिहरति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now when the Sun has set they walk out of the sadas by the eastern door and sit in front of the Havirdhāna. While they sit observing silence, the Pratiprasthātā silently goes round (them) pouring the Vasatīvari waters. (This is) for whatever desire they get initiated.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथा᳘स्तमित आदित्ये प्रा᳘ञ्चो निःसृप्या᳘ग्रेण हविर्धा᳘नमासते ता᳘न्वाचंयमा᳘नेव᳘ वाचंयमः᳘ प्रतिप्रस्थाता᳘ वसतीव᳘रीभिः प᳘रिहरति

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/12 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्यो त᳓स्य ज᳓पो [[भू᳓र्भुवः|भू᳓र्भु᳓वः]] स्व᳓रि᳓ति त᳓द्वा᳓चं सत्ये᳓न स᳓मर्धयति ।

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"May we be abundantly bestowed with heroes"—thereby they pray for heroes. "May we be abundantly bestowed with nourishing food"—thereby they pray for nourishment. Thus from these, all these wishes are fulfilled.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘द्वीराना᳘शास्ते सुपो᳘षः पा᳘षैरि᳘ति तत्पु᳘ष्टिमा᳘शास्त एव᳘मस्मा एता᳘ आशि᳘षः स᳘मृध्यन्ते ॥२१॥
</details>

## २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/38 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ गृह᳓पतिः सुब्रह्मण्या᳓मा᳓ह्वयति यं᳓ वा गृह᳓पतिर्ब्रूया᳓त्स᳓र्वे ह त्वे᳓क आ᳓ह्वयन्ति [[गृह᳓पतिस्त्वे᳙वा᳓ह्वयेद्यं|गृह᳓पतिस्त्वे᳙वा᳓ह्वयेद्यं᳓]] वा गृह᳓पतिर्ब्रूया᳓त्त᳓स्मिन्नुपहाव᳓मिच्छ᳓न्ते᳓ऽथ समि᳓धोऽभ्या᳓दधत्येत᳓दै᳓वैत᳓त्क᳓र्मान्ततः᳓ स᳓मिन्धते ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then the Gṛhapati recites the Subrahmaṇya litany. Or one authorised by the Gṛhapati recites. Sometimes all of them recite, each separately. (That is not to be done). Gṛhapati alone should recite or the one whom the Gṛhapati authorises. (On that occasion) they wish (to be called) for another sacrifice. They place samidhas (on the fire). This kindling marks the end of the ritual. (Third Brāhmaṇa Ends)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ गृह᳘पतिः सुब्रह्मण्यामा᳘ह्वयति यं᳘ वा गृह᳘पतिर्ब्रूयात्स᳘र्वे ह त्वे᳘क आ᳘ह्वयन्ति गृह᳘पति᳘स्त्वेवा᳘ह्वयेद्यं वा गृह᳘पतिर्ब्रूयात्त᳘स्मिन्नुपहाव᳘मिच्छन्ते᳘ऽथ समि᳘धोऽभ्या᳘दधत्येत᳘दै᳘वैतत्क᳘र्मान्ततः स᳘मिन्धते ॥२२॥
</details>
